# Clock variants

[catalog index](../CATALOG.md)

A variant is a clock with the same period as a smaller clock (its host: fewer blocks) whose block kinds it all uses, plus others; the galleries show only the hosts. 1478 variants of 385 hosts, by period. Generated by `mctas clock catalog`.
